## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，我会开一个任务去查，弄清楚了告诉你。
```

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a failing test in one of your repos fixed on its own branch and tell you when it's clear.
```

### greet-ko

```

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を保証する通信」、UDPは「速さ優先で届けっぱなし（保証なし）の通信」です。

TCPは届いたか・順番どおりかを確認し、届かなければ再送します。UDPはそうした確認をせず、軽くて速いのが特徴です。動画配信やオンラインゲーム、DNSなどはUDPを使うことが多いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales, que aún no has compartido, antes de integrarlos. Usa merge para unir ramas que otros ya usan, porque reescribir historia pública rompe el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、可靠有序，像打电话，确保数据完整送达；UDP 无连接、不保证送达，像寄明信片，胜在快和开销小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase to tidy your own local, unshared commits before you push or open a PR. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, since rebasing rewrites history others may have based work on.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to the database on disk before the change is applied to the data files. After a crash, the database replays that log to recover committed work and discard anything half-finished.
```

### rich-compare-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Put these side by side for me. Mac mini M4: $599, 16 GB, always plugged in, stays at home. MacBook Air M4: $999, 16 GB, runs on battery, comes with me. Which is the better always-on home for you?
  [partner message] The Mac mini is the better always-on home for me. It costs $400 less, it's built for being plugged in and left alone, and it stays put.

| | Mac mini M4 | MacBook Air M4 |
|---|---|---|
| Price | $599 | $999 |
| Memory | 16 GB | 16 GB |
| Power | Always plugged in, no battery | Runs on battery, so you'd need to keep its charge limit set and the lid open or an external display attached |
| Wired networking | Built-in Ethernet | Wi-Fi by default |
| Portability | Stays at home | Comes with you |

The Air's only real advantage is that it travels with you. I run at home, so that doesn't help me, and the Mac mini does the job for $400 less.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着 compaction 那个 PR 把锁粒度改细，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 按 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把锁的范围改细，只锁真正需要互斥的部分。
- 改动只在这个任务已有的分支上提交，不要动其他 PR 的分支。
- 跑一遍相关测试，把结果作为证据报告回来。

不做的：
- 不推送到远端，不把草稿 PR 标为 ready，不合并。推送和改 PR 状态等用户点头再做。

完成标准：
- 锁粒度的改动已经提交到分支；
- 相关测试通过，并附上测试输出；
- 简短说明改了哪几处锁、各自缩小到什么范围。
```

  });

}

render();

```

  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 · 合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
  </div>

  <section class="k-card" id="form">
    <div class="k-h" id="formTitle">记一笔</div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>哪天</label>
      <div class="k-chips" id="dayChips"></div>
      <button class="k-btn" id="addDay" type="button">加一天</button>
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="what">花在哪</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如 午饭、地铁、打车">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="amt">金额（元）</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0">
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save" type="button">记下</button>
    <button class="k-btn" id="cancel" type="button" hidden>取消</button>
    <button class="k-btn" id="del" type="button" hidden>删除这一笔</button>
  </section>

  <div id="list"></div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
  var S = { days: 1, cur: 1, items: [], edit: null };

  function fmt(n) {
    return '¥' + Number(n).toLocaleString('zh … tion (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest('.k-chip');
    if (!b) return;
    S.cur = Number(b.getAttribute('data-day'));
    render();
  });

  // tap a row to change it
  $('list').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    startEdit(Number(row.getAttribute('data-i')));
  });

  $('addDay').addEventListener('click', function () {
    S.days += 1;
    S.cur = S.days;
    persist();
    render();
  });

  $('save').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var what = $('what').value.trim();
    var amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!what || !isFinite(amount) || amount < 0) return;
    if (S.edit !== null) {
      S.items[S.edit] = { day: S.cur, what: what, amount: amount };
    } else {
      S.items.push({ day: S.cur, what: what, amount: amount });
    }
    resetForm();
    persist();
    render();
  });

  $('cancel').addEventListener('click', function () { resetForm(); render(); });

  $('del').addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (S.edit === null) return;
    S.items.splice(S.edit, 1);
    resetForm();
    persist();
    render();
  });

  ducter.onChange(reload);
  reload();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

### app-edit-zh

```
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 加个合计，放在最上面，大一点
  [partner message] 合计已经放在最上面，字号也大了。目前北京出差一共 ¥1,966，3 笔。
  [an app card under the message: changed "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2299 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记一笔，记下来的会列在这里。</div>
</div>
<details class="k-add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" value="2026-11-23"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const fmt=n=>"¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US");
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  const t=document.getElementById("total");
  const c=document.getElementById("count");
  if(xs.length){t.textContent=fmt(total);c.textContent=xs.length+" 笔";}
  else{t.innerHTML='<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';c.textContent="记了就会合计在这里";}
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.append(main,sub,val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
  document.getElementById("empty").hidden=xs.length>0;
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
}
document.getElementById("add").onclick=()=>{
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim();
  if(!what||!(yuan>=0))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what,yuan,day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
  document.querySelector(".k-add").open=false;
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

<details class="k-add" id="form">
  <summary>Add an expense</summary>

    var amt=parseFloat(document.getElementById('amt').value.replace(',','.'));
    if(!(amt>0))return;
    var d=new Date();

    items.push({id:Date.now(),amount:Math.round(amt*100)/100,cat:st.cat,note:document.getElementById('note').value.trim(),date:ymd(d)});

      save();
    });
  };

      items=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];
      render();
    });

})();

.k-sum-value{font-size:40px;font-weight:600;margin:4px 0;color:var(--d-accent)}
.k-sum-value small{font-size:16px;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-none{font-size:24px;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-sum-sub{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-soft)}
.k-h{font-size:14px;color:var(--d-muted);margin:12px 4px 6px;font-weight:500}
.k-card{background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:14px;padding:4px 14px}
.k-list{display:flex;flex-direction:column}
.k-row{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:12px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--d-line);flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;cursor:pointer}
.k-row:last-child{border-bottom:none}
.k-row-main{font-size:17px}
.k-row-value{font-size:17px;font-weight:600}
.k-row-del{width:100%;display:flex;justify-content:flex-end}
.k-empty{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:14px;padding:14px 0}
.k-add{margin-top:14px;background:var(--d- … value">'+f.ml+' ml</div>';
    const wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='k-row-del';
    wrap.hidden=openId!==f.id;
    const del=document.createElement('button');
    del.className='k-btn';
    del.textContent='删除这一次';
    del.onclick=async e=>{e.stopPropagation();const cur=(await ducter.get('feeds'))||[];const next=cur.filter(x=>x.id!==f.id);await ducter.set('feeds',next);openId=null;render(next);};
    wrap.appendChild(del);
    row.appendChild(wrap);
    row.onclick=()=>{openId=openId===f.id?null:f.id;render(all);};
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(rows.length?'今天 '+rows.length+' 次,合计 '+total+' ml':'今天还没记');
}
$('add').onclick=async()=>{
  const m=$('time').value.trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
  const ml=parseFloat($('ml').value);
  if(!m||!(ml>0))return;
  const time=pad(+m[1])+':'+m[2];
  const all=(await ducter.get('feeds'))||[];
  all.push({id:Date.now(),date:todayKey(),time,ml});
  await ducter.set('feeds',all);
  $('ml').value='';
  render(all);
};
$('time').value=pad(new Date().getHours())+':'+pad(new Date().getMinutes());
(async()=>{
  render((await ducter.get('feeds'))||[]);
  ducter.onChange(v=>render(v||[]));
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"date":"2026-11-23","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"date":"2026-11-23","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Your PR page is ready: four rows, and tapping one ticks it off as landed. It keeps its ticks between visits, and the card shows how many are still waiting.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1758 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div><div class="k-sum-sub">Tap a PR to tick it off when it lands</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<script>
var items = ducter.get("items") || [];
var done = {};
function render() {
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.textContent = "";
  var open = 0;
  items.forEach(function (it) {
    var isDone = !!done[it.id];
    if (!isDone) open++;
    var row = document.createElement("button");
    row.className = "k-row";
    row.style.width = "100%";
    row.style.textAlign = "left";
    row.style.background = "transparent";
    row.style.border = "0";
    row.style.color = "inherit";
    row.style.cursor = "pointer";
    row.style.opacity = isDone ? "0.5" : "1";
    var box = document.createElement("span");
    box.textContent = isDone ? "☑ " : "☐ ";
    var main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = it.title;
    if (isDone) main.style.textDecoration = "line-through";
    var sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = isDone ? "Landed" : it.sub;
    row.appendChild(box);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    row.addEventListener("click", function () {
      done[it.id] = !done[it.id];
      ducter.set("done", done);
      render();
    });
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = String(open);
  ducter.summary(open + " waiting");
}
function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("done")).then(function (v) {
    done = v || {};
    render();
  });
}
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>记了第一笔，这里就会列出来。</div>

<details class="k-add" id="add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>

    <div class="k-field"><label for="trip">出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="比如 北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="day">日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>分类</label>
      <div class="k-chips" id="cats">
        <button class="k-chip" data-c="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button>
        <button class="k-chip" data-c="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button>
        <button class="k-chip" data-c="餐饮" aria-pressed="false">餐饮</button>
        <button class="k-chip" data-c="其他" aria-pressed="false">其他</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="amt">金额（元）</label><input class="k-input k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
    <button class= …     '<button class="k-row" data-i="'+i+'"><span class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.trip)+' · '+esc(x.cat||"交通")+'</span><span class="k-row-sub">'+esc([x.day,x.note].filter(Boolean).join(" · "))+'</span><span class="k-row-value">'+fmt(x.amt)+'</span></button>'
  ).join("");
  $("empty").hidden=items.length>0;
  document.querySelectorAll(".k-row").forEach(r=>r.onclick=()=>{
    const i=Number(r.dataset.i),x=items[i];edit=i;
    $("trip").value=x.trip||"";$("day").value=x.day||"";$("amt").value=x.amt??"";setCat(x.cat||"交通");
    $("save").textContent="改好了";$("del").hidden=false;$("add").open=true;
  });
  ducter.summary(items.length?items.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
}

function load(){items=ducter.get(K)||[];render()}

$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number($("amt").value);
  if(!$("amt").value||isNaN(amt))return;
  const rec={trip:$("trip").value.trim()||"未命名出差",day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:amt,note:edit>=0?(items[edit].note||""):""};
  if(edit>=0)items[edit]=rec;else items.push(rec);
  ducter.set(K,items);reset();render();
};
$("del").onclick=()=>{
  if(edit<0)return;
  items.splice(edit,1);ducter.set(K,items);reset();render();
};
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让修 TTL 测试的那个任务再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，补完告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户的原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

在现有的假时钟测试基础上，再加一条测试，覆盖 TTL 正好到期的那一刻：
- 时钟推进到恰好等于过期时间时，值应当已经视为过期（或按你们代码里既定的边界语义，明确断言边界行为）。
- 时钟比过期时间少一毫秒（或最小单位）时，值仍然有效。
- 继续使用假时钟，不要引入真实 sleep 或计时。

完成标准：新测试通过，并把运行结果（测试名和通过数）报告回来；现有 TTL 测试保持通过。
范围外：不要合并到主分支，不要推送，不要开 PR。修改留在现有的任务分支上提交。
工作目录：~/dev/foo
```
